### ENTERING NOW INTO THE REALIZATION OF THE HIGHEST RICHES.

I hear the question: what can be said in a concrete way regarding the method of achieving this realization? The facts underlying it are, indeed, most beautiful and true, but how can we <term>actualize</term><note>actualize: To make something a reality in one's personal experience.</note> in ourselves the realization that carries with it such wonderful results?

The method is not difficult if we do not make it difficult for ourselves. The principal word to be used is the word — *Open*. Simply open your mind and heart to this <term>divine inflow</term><note>divine inflow: The idea that spiritual energy or God's presence flows into the individual like a stream.</note> which is waiting only for the opening of the gate so that it may enter. It is like opening the gate of the channel that carries water from a reservoir above into the field below. By its very nature, the water will rush in and irrigate the field if the gate is simply opened. As for the realization of our oneness with this <term>Infinite Life</term><note>Infinite Life: A term often used in New Thought philosophy to describe a universal, divine energy or God that permeates all things.</note> and Power—after seeing, as I think we have clearly seen by this time, the relationship it has with us and we with it—the main thing to be said is simply: *realize your oneness with it.* The

An open mind and heart, through which one is brought into a <term>receptive attitude</term><note>receptive attitude: A state of being open and ready to receive spiritual influence or insight.</note>, is the first thing necessary. Then comes the earnest, sincere desire.

It may be an aid at first to take yourself into the quiet for a few moments each day—into <term>the silence</term><note>the silence: A meditative state of inward stillness common in New Thought and mystical traditions, used to connect with the divine.</note>—where you will not be agitated by the disturbances that enter through the avenues of the physical senses. There in the quiet, alone with God, put yourself into a receptive state. Calmly, quietly, and expectantly desire that this realization break in upon and take possession of your soul. As it breaks in upon and takes possession of the soul, it will manifest itself to your mind, and from this, you will feel its manifestations in every part of your body. 

Then, in the degree that you open yourself to it, you will feel a quiet, peaceful, illuminating power that will harmonize body, soul, and mind, and that will then harmonize these with all the world. You are now on the <term>mountain top</term><note>mountain top: A metaphor for a high state of spiritual clarity or a "peak experience" of divine connection.</note>, and the voice of God is speaking to you. *Then, as you descend, carry this realization with you.* Live in it while waking, working, thinking, walking, and sleeping. In this way, although you may not be continually on the mountain top, you will nevertheless be continually living in the realization of all the beauty, and inspiration, and power you have felt there.

->217<-

# Fullness of Peace, Power, and Plenty

regarding <note>original: "gard to"; this completes the word "regard" from the previous page's catchword "re-".</note> what to do or how to do it. Their lives are carefree <note>original: "care-less"; in this context, the author means "free from anxiety or worry" rather than "negligent" or "thoughtless."</note> lives. They are lives free from worry because they are continually conscious of the fact that the higher powers are doing the guiding, and they are relieved of the responsibility. To describe these lives in detail—and particularly two or three that come to my mind at this moment—would reveal facts that some would no doubt find almost incredible, if not miraculous. 

But let us remember that what is possible for one life to realize is possible for all. This is indeed the natural and the normal life; it is the everyday life of everyone who enters into and lives in this higher realization, and so lives in harmony with the higher laws. This is simply getting into the current of that divine sequence <note>divine sequence: A concept in New Thought spirituality referring to the orderly, purposeful flow of the universe directed by a higher power.</note> running throughout the universe. Once you are in it, life ceases to be a struggle and moves along day after day much as the tides flow, much as the planets move in their orbits, or much as the seasons come and go.

All the friction, all the uncertainty, all the illness, the suffering, the fear, the dread, and the confusion of life come to us because we are out of harmony with the divine order of things. They will continue to come as long as we live that way. Rowing against the tide is hard.

<vocab>higher powers, higher realization, higher laws, divine sequence, divine order, harmony</vocab>

<summary>The author explains that a life lived in harmony with divine laws becomes effortless and "carefree," moving with the natural flow of the universe rather than struggling against it.</summary>
<keywords>divine guidance, harmony, spiritual laws, New Thought, divine sequence, carefree living, universal order</keywords>

->218<-

# Fullness of Peace, Power, and Plenty

and uncertain. To go with the tide, and thus take advantage of the working of a great natural force, is safe and easy. To come into the conscious, vital realization of our oneness <note>oneness: a core concept in New Thought and mystical traditions referring to the essential unity between the individual soul and the divine Creator.</note> with the Infinite Life and Power is to come into the current of this divine sequence. Coming into harmony with the Infinite in this way brings us, in turn, into harmony with everything around us—into harmony with the life of the heavens and into harmony with the entire universe. Above all, it brings us into harmony with ourselves, so that body, soul, and mind become perfectly harmonized. When this is achieved, life becomes full and complete.

The life of the senses then no longer masters and enslaves us. The physical is subordinated to and ruled by the mental; this, in turn, is subordinated to and continually illuminated by the spiritual. Life is then no longer the poor, one-sided thing it is in so many cases. Instead, the threefold, all-around life—with all its beauties and ever-increasing joys and powers—is entered upon. 

Thus it is that we are brought to realize that the middle path <note>middle path: a way of life that avoids the extremes of both self-denial and self-indulgence.</note> is the great solution of life; it is neither asceticism <term>asceticism: severe self-discipline and the avoidance of all forms of indulgence, often for religious reasons</term> on one hand, nor license <note>original: "license"; in this context, it refers to a lack of restraint or the abuse of freedom.</note> and perverted use on the other. Everything is intended for use, but all must be wisely used in order to be fully enjoyed.

<summary>The author explains that aligning oneself with the "Infinite" creates harmony across the body, mind, and spirit, leading to a balanced life that avoids the extremes of self-denial and overindulgence.</summary>
<keywords>oneness, Infinite Life, divine sequence, harmony, threefold life, middle path, asceticism, balance</keywords>

--- Page 225 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the benefits of living a balanced, spiritual life, moving from the concept of the "middle path" to the refinement of the senses and the attainment of direct spiritual knowledge.</meta>

### Fullness of Peace, Power, and Plenty 219

As we live in these higher realizations <note>realizations: in this context, the act of making spiritual truths a conscious reality in one's life.</note>, the senses are not ignored but are ever more fully perfected. As the body becomes less dense and heavy—finer in its texture and form—all the senses become more refined, so that powers we do not currently realize belong to us gradually develop. Thus, we come into the super-conscious realms in a perfectly natural and normal way, making it possible for higher laws and truths to be revealed to us. 

As we enter these realms, we are no longer among those who spend their time speculating whether this or that person had the insight and the powers attributed to them; instead, we are able to *know* for ourselves. Nor are we among those who attempt to lead people based on the hearsay of someone else. Instead, we know what we are talking about, and only in this way can we speak with authority. There are many things that we cannot know until, by living the spiritual life, we bring ourselves into a state where it is possible for them to be revealed to us. “If anyone is willing to do God’s <note>original: "His"</note> will, he shall know whether the teaching <note>original: "doctrine"; a reference to the Gospel of John 7:17</note> is from God.” 

It was Plotinus <note>Plotinus (c. 204–270 AD): A major Greek-speaking philosopher of late antiquity and the founder of Neoplatonism.</note> who said, “The mind that wishes to behold God must itself become God.” As we make it possible for these higher laws and truths to be revealed to us, we will in turn become enlightened ones—channels through which these truths may be revealed to others.

<summary>This page explains how spiritual living refines the physical senses and leads to "super-conscious" states, allowing for direct personal knowledge of divine truths rather than relying on hearsay.</summary>
<keywords>super-consciousness, spiritual growth, Plotinus, divine revelation, enlightenment, inner knowledge, sensory refinement</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the spiritual person's impact on their environment, following the previous page's focus on how spiritual refinement allows a person to speak with authority and reveal truths to others.</meta>

# Fullness of Peace, Power, and Plenty 220

When one is fully alive to the possibilities that come with this higher awakening <note>higher awakening: the state of becoming spiritually conscious or enlightened.</note>, as he goes here and there and mingles with his fellow human beings, he imparts to all an inspiration that kindles in them a feeling of power similar <note>original: "kindred"</note> to his own. We are all continually giving out influences similar to those that are active in our own lives. We do this in the same way that each flower gives off its own unique scent. The rose breathes out its fragrance into the air, and all who come near it are refreshed and inspired by this emanation <note>emanation: something that flows out from a source, like scent from a flower or light from a star.</note> from the soul of the rose. A poisonous weed sends out its unpleasant <note>original: "obnoxious"; in this context, it means harmful or offensive.</note> odor; it is neither refreshing nor inspiring in its effects, and if one remains near it for long, he may be so unpleasantly affected as to even be made ill by it.

The higher the life, the more inspiring and helpful are the influences <note>original: "emanations"</note> that it is continually sending out. The lower the life, the more harmful is the influence it continually sends out to all who come in contact with it. Each person is continually radiating an atmosphere of one kind or the other.

We are told by the sailors <note>original: "mariners"</note> who sail on the Indian Seas <note>Indian Seas: the Indian Ocean and surrounding waters, historically famous for the trade of fragrant woods and spices.</note> that they are often able to tell they are approaching certain islands long before they can see them, thanks to the sweet fragrance of the sandalwood that is wafted far out upon the...

<summary>This page explains that every person radiates a spiritual influence or "atmosphere" that affects others, comparing the inspiring presence of an enlightened soul to the fragrance of a rose or sandalwood.</summary>
<keywords>spiritual influence, emanation, higher awakening, atmosphere, sandalwood, Indian Seas</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the exploration of the spiritual atmosphere a person radiates, moving from the metaphor of floral scents on the previous page to the tangible impact a spiritually "translucent" person has on the people and world around them.</meta>

# Fullness of Peace, Power, and Plenty 221

...deep. Do you not see how beneficial it would be to have such a soul expressing itself through such a body? As you go here and there, a subtle, silent force emanates from you that everyone feels and is influenced by. You carry an inspiration with you and continually offer a blessing <note>original: "benediction"</note> wherever you go. Your friends and all people will say, "His arrival brings peace and joy into our homes; welcome his coming." 

As you pass along the street, people who are tired, weary, and even those who are spiritually burdened <note>original: "sin-sick"</note> will feel a certain divine touch that will awaken new desires and a new life within them. It will even make a horse turn its head as you pass, looking at you with a strange, almost human, longing look. 

Such are the subtle powers of the human soul when it allows the Divine to shine through it <note>original: "translucent to the Divine"</note>. To know that such a life is possible for us right here and now is enough to make anyone burst into songs of joy. And when you actually begin to live this life, the sentiment of at least one song will be:

> ->“Oh! I stand in the Great Forever,<-

> ->I drink of the heavenly wine.<-
>
> ->“In the gleam of the shining rainbow<-
> ->The Father’s Love I behold,<-
> ->As I gaze on its radiant blending<-
> ->Of crimson and blue and gold."<-

# Fullness of Peace, Power, and Plenty 222

> ->"In all the bright birds that are singing,<-
> ->In all the fair flowers that bloom,<-
> ->Whose welcome aromas are bringing<-
> ->Their blessings of sweet perfume;<-
>
> ->"In the glorious tint of the morning,<-
> ->In the gorgeous sheen of the night,<-
> ->Oh! my soul is lost in rapture,<-
> ->My senses are lost in sight."<-

As a person comes into and lives continually in the full, conscious realization of their <note>original: "his"</note> oneness with the Infinite Life and Power <note>Infinite Life and Power: a term used in New Thought philosophy to describe God or the universal source of all existence.</note>, then everything else follows. This is what brings the realization of such splendors, beauties, and joys that only a life connected with the Infinite Power can know. This is what it means to experience heaven’s richest treasures while still walking upon the earth. This brings heaven down to earth—or rather, it lifts the earth up to heaven. It means exchanging weakness and powerlessness <note>original: "impotence"</note> for strength; sorrows and sighs for joy; fears and anxieties <note>original: "forebodings"</note> for faith; and longings for fulfillment <note>original: "realizations"</note>. This is how one reaches the fullness of peace, power, and plenty. This is what it means to be in tune with the Infinite.

## By RALPH WALDO TRINE.

->“The Life Books.”<-

### What All the World’s A-Seeking.

> Its purpose is distinctly practical. It is written most fascinatingly and deserves the remarkable success it has achieved. — *The Review of Reviews.*

> The volume abounds in passages of great beauty and strength; but the striking feature of the book is, after all, the solid, sensible, healthy explanation <note>original: "exposition"</note> of the single theme it is written to promote <note>original: "enforce"</note>. — *New York Independent.*

> This is a book among a thousand for its inspiring message, and it is eminently worthy of a vast audience. You will miss much if you miss reading its pages, which are filled with truth <note>original: "truth-laden"</note>. — *Cumberland Presbyterian.*

### In Tune With the Infinite.

> It is one of the simplest, clearest works ever written, dealing with the power of inner <note>original: "interior"</note> forces in shaping <note>original: "moulding"</note> the everyday conditions of life. — *San Francisco Bulletin.*

> . . . It immediately suggests the works of Drummond <note>Henry Drummond (1851–1897) was a Scottish naturalist and evangelist who sought to reconcile science with Christianity; he was a major influence on the New Thought movement.</note>, but shows a decided advance upon the ground which he made familiar to mankind; it not only reveals the author’s recognition of spiritual law, but in certain instances shows his rare and remarkable understanding of the nature and action of such law. The preface alone shows his high intuition, and the book proves his ability to achieve his purpose in a marvelous degree. — *Boston Daily Evening Transcript.*

> Mr. Trine can write well upon such topics as this. He is energetic <note>original: "alive"</note>, vigorous, cheerful <note>original: "cheery"</note>, and confident. The work has a distinctiveness in its style and method. — *The Literary World, London.*

## By RALPH WALDO TRINE.

---

### The "Life" Booklets.

# The Greatest Thing Ever Known.

The moment we fully and vitally realize *who and what we are*, we then begin to build our own world even as God builds His. — *From the Title page.*

. . . It reveals <note>original: "unfolds"</note> the secret of our underlying strength, and shows what it is that gives us power to fulfill <note>original: "fulfil"</note> the real and living purposes of our being. — *The New Christianity.*

# Every Living Creature.

The tender and humane passion in the human heart is too precious a quality to allow it to be hardened or wiped out <note>original: "effaced"</note> by practices such as we so often indulge in. — *From the Title page.*

An eloquent appeal and an able argument for justice and mercy to our animal <note>original: "dumb"</note> fellow-creatures. A good book for those whose characters are being formed, and for all who love justice and right. — *Religio-Philosophical Journal.*

# Character-Building Thought Power.

A thought, good or evil, leads to an act, and in time a habit—so runs life's law; what you live in your thought-world, you will find made real <note>original: "objectified"</note> in your life sooner or later. — *From the Title page.*

In "Character-Building Thought Power," Mr. Trine demonstrates the power of mental habits, and shows how by daily effort we may train ourselves into right ways of thinking and acting. His teachings are sound, practical, and of priceless worth. — *Albany Press.*
